Let n even L=2M. For each pair t=0..M-1 (indices t and L-1-t), variables u_t≥0 (pair mass) and r_t∈[0,1] orientation: a_t = r_t u_t, a_{L-1-t}= (1-r_t)u_t. Sum all a =∑u_t =1. Objective coefficients:

def pair_fg(v, n, tau, N, idx1, idx2):
 M=n//2; Mv=M-1

def optimize_pair_start(n, v0, deadline, label=""):
 M=n//2; N=next_pow2(3*n-2); idx1, idx2.
 v=v0.copy(); clip to bounds.
 best_a=pair_sequence(v,n); best_score=internal_score(best_a)
 tau_fracs=(0.18,0.12,0.08,0.05,0.03,0.02,0.012,0.008,0.005)
 for tf in tau_fracs:
   if time>deadline: break
   a_cur=pair_sequence(v,n); cmax=...; tau=max(tf*cmax,1e-12)
   maxiter = 80 if n<=2048 else 40
   maxfun = 800 if n<=2048 else 300
   try:
     res=opt.minimize(pair_fg, v, args=(n,tau,N,idx1,idx2), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxfun,'maxls':8,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-8})
     if res.x is not None and finite: v=res.x
   except Exception: pass
   v=clip(v, bounds)
   a=pair_sequence(v,n); sc=internal_score(a,N?) internal uses N? We'll call internal_score(a) its own N maybe 2n-1. If improve update.
   print if improve.
 # final line search? Maybe try tau very small with current v by evaluating score only.
 return best_a,best_score

def local_polish(a, deadline, seed=0):

 n; N=next_pow2(2*n-1) maybe for score; best_score

   width = int(rng.integers(max(2,n//200), min(max(3,n//10), n//2)+1))
   start=...
   old=loga[start:start+width].copy()
   d=sigma*rng.normal(size=width); d-=d.mean(); d=np.clip(d,-3,3); env maybe.
   loga[start:start+width]+=d
   a_try=exp(clip(loga-max,-50,0)); normalize; score=internal_score(a_try,N)
   if score<best: update loga, sigma increase
   else revert, sigma decrease, stale.

compute_weights:
def compute_weights(c,cmax,tau,floor=0.02):
 if invalid uniform
 z=(c-cmax)/tau; clip; e; s; w=e/s
 if floor: w=(1-floor)w+floor/len
 return w

def make_initial_fast(n, seed, max_count=10):
 rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
 x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
 seqs=[np.ones(n)/n]
 for alpha in (0.25,0.5,0.75,-0.25,-0.5,-0.75):
   vals=1+alpha*np.sin(np.pi*x); seqs.append(normalize_positive(vals))
 for mu in (0.3,0.7): for sig in (0.12,0.25): seqs.append(normalize_positive(np.exp(-((x-mu)**2)/(2sig*sig))))
 for slope in (-2,2): seqs.append(normalize_positive(np.exp(slope*(x-0.5))))
 for _ in range(2): vals=np.exp(rng.normal(size=n)*0.5); seqs.append(normalize_positive(vals))
 if len>max_count: keep first max. Return.

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative step-function heights minimizing

    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2

using a complementary-pair reparameterization:

For even n = 2M, pair index i with n-1-i:
    a_i       = r_i * u_i
    a_{n-1-i} = (1-r_i) * u_i

where u_i > 0, sum_i u_i = 1, and r_i in [0,1].
The optimizer works on log-ratios of u_i and logits of r_i.  It
minimizes a smoothed log-sum-exp of the convolution coefficients with
L-BFGS-B, followed by mirror-descent and local block polishing.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    import scipy.optimize as _scipy_optimize
except Exception:
    _scipy_optimize = None

# Pair parameter scaling / bounds.  The code uses yv variables where
# y_ext = [YS * yv_0, ..., YS * yv_{M-2}, 0], so the last u is the
# reference and the gauge freedom is removed.
Y_SCALE = 2.0
Y_BOUND = 7.0
Z_BOUND = 15.0


def next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (int(m) - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_positive(a, floor=0.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, float(floor), None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def convolve_seq(a, N=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if N is None:
        N = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    else:
        need = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
        if int(N) < int(need):
            N = need
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[: 2 * n - 1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def internal_score(a, N=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
        return np.inf
    a = a / s
    c = convolve_seq(a, N)
    return float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(c)))


def convolve_and_fft(a, N=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if N is None:
        N = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    else:
        need = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
        if int(N) < int(need):
            N = need
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[: 2 * n - 1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c, fa


def grad_from_weights(w, fa, N, n):
    # Returns gradient of sum_k w_k (a*a)_k with respect to a.
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w_rev = w[::-1]
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w_rev, N)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fa * fw, N)
    return 2.0 * conv[n - 1 : 2 * n - 1][::-1]


def smooth_value(c, cmax, tau):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    cmax = float(cmax)
    tau = float(tau)
    if c.size == 0 or cmax <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(cmax) or tau <= 0.0:
        return 0.0
    z = (c - cmax) / tau
    z = np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return cmax
    return float(cmax + tau * np.log(s))


def compute_weights(c, cmax, tau, floor=0.02):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    L = c.size
    if L == 0:
        return c
    cmax = float(cmax)
    tau = float(tau)
    if cmax <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(cmax) or tau <= 0.0:
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64)
    z = (c - cmax) / tau
    z = np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64)
    w = e / s
    if floor > 0.0:
        w = (1.0 - float(floor)) * w + (float(floor) / float(L))
    return w


def resample_seq(a, n):
    a = normalize_positive(a, floor=0.0)
    old_n = a.size
    n = max(2, int(n))
    if old_n == 1:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    if old_n == n:
        return a.astype(np.float64, copy=True)
    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n, dtype=np.float64)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.interp(new_x, old_x, a)
    return normalize_positive(b, floor=1e-12)


def get_given_sequence():
    try:
        seq = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if seq is None:
            return None
        a = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if a.size == 0:
            return None
        a = normalize_positive(a, floor=0.0)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
            return None
        return a
    except Exception:
        return None


def make_initial_fast(n, seed, max_count=8):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    n = max(8, int(n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    seqs = [np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)]

    for alpha in (0.25, 0.50, 0.75, -0.25, -0.50, -0.75):
        vals = 1.0 + alpha * np.sin(np.pi * x)
        seqs.append(normalize_positive(vals, floor=1e-12))

    for mu in (0.32, 0.68):
        for sig in (0.12, 0.24):
            vals = np.exp(-((x - mu) ** 2) / (2.0 * sig * sig))
            seqs.append(normalize_positive(vals, floor=1e-12))

    for slope in (-1.8, 1.8):
        vals = np.exp(slope * (x - 0.5))
        seqs.append(normalize_positive(vals, floor=1e-12))

    # V-shaped / U-shaped probes.
    for beta in (0.35, 0.70):
        vals = (1.0 - beta * np.abs(1.0 - 2.0 * x)) ** 2
        seqs.append(normalize_positive(vals, floor=1e-12))

    for _ in range(2):
        vals = np.exp(0.55 * rng.normal(size=n))
        seqs.append(normalize_positive(vals, floor=1e-12))

    if max_count is not None and len(seqs) > int(max_count):
        keep = list(range(min(6, len(seqs))))
        rest = list(range(len(keep), len(seqs)))
        rng.shuffle(rest)
        keep.extend(rest[: max(0, int(max_count) - len(keep))])
        seqs = [seqs[i] for i in keep]
    return seqs


def pair_sequence(yv, z, n):
    """Convert pair variables to a nonnegative length-n sequence."""
    n = int(n)
    M = n // 2
    if M < 1:
        return normalize_positive(np.ones(n), floor=0.0)

    yv = np.asarray(yv, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if yv.size != M - 1:
        yv = np.clip(yv, -50.0, 50.0)
        if yv.size < M - 1:
            yv = np.concatenate((yv, np.zeros(max(0, M - 1 - yv.size))))
        elif yv.size > M - 1:
            yv = yv[: M - 1]
    if z.size != M:
        z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
        if z.size < M:
            z = np.concatenate((z, np.zeros(max(0, M - z.size))))
        elif z.size > M:
            z = z[:M]

    y_ext = np.empty(M, dtype=np.float64)
    if M - 1 > 0:
        y_ext[:-1] = np.clip(yv, -50.0, 50.0) * Y_SCALE
    y_ext[-1] = 0.0

    y_ext = np.nan_to_num(y_ext, nan=0.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
    y_shift = y_ext - float(np.max(y_ext))
    U = np.exp(np.clip(y_shift, -60.0, 0.0))
    su = float(U.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(su) or su <= 0.0:
        U = np.full(M, 1.0 / float(M), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        U = U / su

    zc = np.clip(np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=25.0, neginf=-25.0), -25.0, 25.0)
    r = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-zc))

    idx1 = np.arange(M, dtype=np.int64)
    idx2 = n - 1 - idx1
    a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[idx1] = r * U
    a[idx2] = (1.0 - r) * U
    return normalize_positive(a, floor=0.0)


def pair_project(a, n):
    """Project a nonnegative sequence of length n into pair variables."""
    a = normalize_positive(a, floor=0.0)
    n = int(n)
    M = n // 2
    idx1 = np.arange(M, dtype=np.int64)
    idx2 = n - 1 - idx1

    u = a[idx1] + a[idx2]
    u = np.maximum(u, 1e-18)
    r = a[idx1] / u
    r = np.clip(r, 1e-9, 1.0 - 1e-9)

    logu = np.log(u)
    if M > 1:
        yv = (logu[: M - 1] - logu[-1]) / Y_SCALE
    else:
        yv = np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    yv = np.clip(np.nan_to_num(yv, nan=0.0, posinf=Y_BOUND, neginf=-Y_BOUND),
                 -Y_BOUND, Y_BOUND)

    z = np.log(r / (1.0 - r))
    z = np.clip(np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=Z_BOUND, neginf=-Z_BOUND),
                -Z_BOUND, Z_BOUND)

    return np.concatenate((yv, z)).astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def pair_fg(v, n, tau, N, idx1, idx2):
    """
    Objective and gradient for smoothed max convolution.

    v layout: [yv_0 ... yv_{M-2} | z_0 ... z_{M-1}]
    """
    n = int(n)
    M = n // 2
    Mv = max(1, M - 1)

    if v.size != n - 1:
        v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if v.size < n - 1:
            v = np.concatenate((v, np.zeros(n - 1 - v.size)))
        else:
            v = v[: n - 1]

    yv = v[:Mv]
    z = v[Mv:]

    y_ext = np.empty(M, dtype=np.float64)
    if M - 1 > 0:
        y_ext[:-1] = np.clip(yv, -50.0, 50.0) * Y_SCALE
    y_ext[-1] = 0.0
    y_ext = np.nan_to_num(y_ext, nan=0.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
    y_shift = y_ext - float(np.max(y_ext))
    U = np.exp(np.clip(y_shift, -60.0, 0.0))
    su = float(U.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(su) or su <= 0.0:
        U = np.full(M, 1.0 / float(M), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        U = U / su

    zc = np.clip(np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=25.0, neginf=-25.0), -25.0, 25.0)
    r = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-zc))

    a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[idx1] = r * U
    a[idx2] = (1.0 - r) * U

    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[: 2 * n - 1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 1e-300:
        return 1.0e100, np.zeros_like(v, dtype=np.float64)

    tau = max(float(tau), 1e-12)
    zeta = (c - cmax) / tau
    zeta = np.clip(zeta, -700.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(zeta)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return 1.0e100, np.zeros_like(v, dtype=np.float64)

    w = e / s
    grad_c = grad_from_weights(w, fa, N, n)
    grad_a = (2.0 * n / tau) * grad_c

    rr = r * (1.0 - r)
    g1 = grad_a[idx1]
    g2 = grad_a[idx2]
    raw_u = r * g1 + (1.0 - r) * g2
    total_u = float(np.dot(U, raw_u))
    grad_yv = Y_SCALE * U[:-1] * (raw_u[:-1] - total_u)
    grad_z = U * rr * (g1 - g2)

    f = (2.0 * n) * (cmax / tau + float(np.log(s)))
    g = np.concatenate((grad_yv, grad_z)).astype(np.float64, copy=False)
    g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=1.0e6, neginf=-1.0e6)
    gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
    if np.isfinite(gmax) and gmax > 1.0e8:
        g *= 1.0e8 / gmax

    return float(f), g


def optimize_pair_start(n, v0, deadline, label=""):
    """Run a short multi-temperature L-BFGS optimization from v0."""
    n = int(n)
    M = n // 2
    Mv = max(1, M - 1)
    idx1 = np.arange(M, dtype=np.int64)
    idx2 = n - 1 - idx1
    N = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

    v = np.asarray(v0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if v.size != n - 1:
        if v.size < n - 1:
            v = np.concatenate((v, np.zeros(n - 1 - v.size)))
        else:
            v = v[: n - 1]
    lows = np.concatenate((np.full(Mv, -Y_BOUND, dtype=np.float64),
                           np.full(M, -Z_BOUND, dtype=np.float64)))
    highs = np.concatenate((np.full(Mv, Y_BOUND, dtype=np.float64),
                            np.full(M, Z_BOUND, dtype=np.float64)))
    v = np.clip(v, lows, highs)

    a0 = pair_sequence(v[:Mv], v[Mv:], n)
    best_a = a0.copy()
    best_score = internal_score(a0)
    print("pair start {} n={} initial={:.9f}".format(label, n, best_score), flush=True)

    if _scipy_optimize is None:
        return best_a, best_score

    if n <= 2048:
        tau_fracs = (0.18, 0.11, 0.065, 0.038, 0.022, 0.013, 0.008)
        maxiter = 32
        maxfun = 110
    else:
        tau_fracs = (0.18, 0.11, 0.065, 0.038, 0.022)
        maxiter = 22
        maxfun = 55

    bounds = [(-Y_BOUND, Y_BOUND)] * Mv + [(-Z_BOUND, Z_BOUND)] * M

    for tf in tau_fracs:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        a_cur = pair_sequence(v[:Mv], v[Mv:], n)
        cmax = float(np.max(convolve_seq(a_cur)))
        tau = max(tf * cmax, 1e-12)

        try:
            res = _scipy_optimize.minimize(
                pair_fg,
                v,
                args=(n, tau, N, idx1, idx2),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                bounds=bounds,
                options={
                    "maxiter": maxiter,
                    "maxfun": maxfun,
                    "maxls": 8,
                    "ftol": 1e-12,
                    "gtol": 1e-8,
                },
            )
            if res is not None and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
                vx = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
                if vx.size == v.size and np.all(np.isfinite(vx)):
                    v = np.clip(vx, lows, highs)
        except Exception:
            pass

        a_cur = pair_sequence(v[:Mv], v[Mv:], n)
        sc = internal_score(a_cur)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score * (1.0 - 1e-12):
            best_score = sc
            best_a = a_cur.copy()
            print("pair {} n={} tau={:.4f} score={:.9f}".format(label, n, tf, best_score), flush=True)

        if best_score < 1.45:
            break

    # Final exact score of the latest point.
    a_final = pair_sequence(v[:Mv], v[Mv:], n)
    sc_final = internal_score(a_final)
    if np.isfinite(sc_final) and sc_final < best_score:
        best_score = sc_final
        best_a = a_final.copy()

    return best_a, best_score


def optimize_mirror(a, deadline, verbose=False):
    """Mirror-descent polishing in the full simplex."""
    a = normalize_positive(a, floor=1e-15)
    n = a.size
    N = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = internal_score(a, N)

    eta = 0.55
    beta = 0.12
    w_floor = 0.03
    stagnant = 0
    it = 0
    last_best = best_score

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c, fa = convolve_and_fft(a, N)
        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        score = float(2.0 * n * cmax)
        if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score * (1.0 - 1e-12):
            best_score = score
            best_a = a.copy()

        if cmax <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(cmax):
            break

        tau = beta * cmax
        w = compute_weights(c, cmax, tau, floor=w_floor)
        g = grad_from_weights(w, fa, N, n)
        g = g - float(np.mean(g))
        scale = float(np.abs(g).max())
        if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <= 1e-15:
            break
        g = g / scale

        loga = np.log(a + 1e-20)
        J_cur = smooth_value(c, cmax, tau)
        accepted = False

        for e in (eta, 0.5 * eta, 0.25 * eta, 0.125 * eta):
            if e <= 1e-10:
                continue
            loga_try = loga - e * g
            loga_try = loga_try - float(np.max(loga_try))
            a_try = np.exp(np.clip(loga_try, -60.0, 0.0))
            a_try = np.maximum(a_try, 1e-300)
            a_try = normalize_positive(a_try, floor=1e-15)

            c_try, fa_try = convolve_and_fft(a_try, N)
            cmax_try = float(np.max(c_try))
            score_try = float(2.0 * n * cmax_try)
            J_try = smooth_value(c_try, cmax_try, tau)

            if np.isfinite(score_try):
                if score_try < score * (1.0 - 1e-10):
                    accepted = True
                    a = a_try
                    score = score_try
                    break
                if np.isfinite(J_try) and J_try < J_cur * (1.0 - 1e-7) and score_try < score * 1.03:
                    accepted = True
                    a = a_try
                    score = score_try
                    break

        if accepted:
            stagnant = 0
            eta = min(1.20, eta * 1.02)
            beta = max(0.005, beta * 0.999)
            w_floor = max(0.0, w_floor * 0.995)
        else:
            stagnant += 1
            eta = max(1e-4, eta * 0.65)
            beta = max(0.004, beta * 0.85)
            if stagnant % 60 == 0:
                beta = min(0.22, beta * 1.6)
                w_floor = min(0.08, w_floor + 0.004)
                stagnant = 0
            if eta < 1e-5 or stagnant > 500:
                break

        it += 1
        if verbose and it % 200 == 0:
            print("mirror n={} iter={} best={:.9f}".format(n, it, best_score), flush=True)

        if it % 500 == 0 and best_score > last_best - 1e-8:
            tmp = a * np.exp(np.clip(np.random.default_rng(it + n).normal(0.0, 0.05, n), -1.0, 1.0))
            tmp = normalize_positive(tmp, floor=1e-15)
            sc_tmp = internal_score(tmp, N)
            if np.isfinite(sc_tmp) and sc_tmp < best_score:
                a = tmp
                best_score = sc_tmp
                best_a = a.copy()
            last_best = best_score

    return best_a, best_score


def local_polish(a, deadline, seed=123):
    """Random log-block perturbations with acceptance only on improvement."""
    a = normalize_positive(a, floor=1e-15)
    n = a.size
    if n < 16 or time.monotonic() >= deadline:
        return a, internal_score(a)

    N = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = internal_score(a, N)

    loga = np.log(a + 1e-20)
    loga = loga - float(np.max(loga))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    sigma = 0.16
    stale = 0

    width_low = max(2, n // 3000)
    width_high = min(n // 2, max(3, n // 28))

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if width_high <= width_low:
            width = max(2, width_low)
        else:
            width = int(rng.integers(width_low, width_high + 1))
        start = int(rng.integers(0, n - width + 1))

        old = loga[start : start + width].copy()
        t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, width, dtype=np.float64)
        env = np.exp(-3.0 * ((t - 0.5) ** 2))
        d = sigma * (0.55 + 0.45 * env) * rng.normal(size=width)
        d = d - float(np.mean(d))
        d = np.clip(d, -3.0, 3.0)

        loga[start : start + width] += d
        a_try = np.exp(np.clip(loga - float(np.max(loga)), -60.0, 0.0))
        a_try = np.maximum(a_try, 1e-300)
        a_try = normalize_positive(a_try, floor=1e-15)
        sc = internal_score(a_try, N)

        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score * (1.0 - 1e-11):
            best_score = sc
            best_a = a_try.copy()
            loga = np.log(a_try + 1e-20)
            loga = loga - float(np.max(loga))
            stale = 0
            sigma = min(0.35, sigma * 1.03)
        else:
            loga[start : start + width] = old
            stale += 1
            sigma = max(0.025, sigma * 0.965)

            if stale > 220:
                idx = int(rng.integers(0, n))
                old_idx = loga[idx]
                loga[idx] += 1.7 * sigma
                a_try = np.exp(np.clip(loga - float(np.max(loga)), -60.0, 0.0))
                a_try = np.maximum(a_try, 1e-300)
                a_try = normalize_positive(a_try, floor=1e-15)
                sc2 = internal_score(a_try, N)
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score:
                    best_score = sc2
                    best_a = a_try.copy()
                    loga = np.log(a_try + 1e-20)
                    loga = loga - float(np.max(loga))
                else:
                    loga[idx] = old_idx
                stale = 0

        if best_score < 1.44:
            break

    return best_a, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2370):
    start = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = float(budget_s) if budget_s is not None else 2370.0
    total_budget = min(2370.0, max(120.0, total_budget))
    deadline = start + max(120.0, total_budget - 12.0)

    n = 2048
    given = get_given_sequence()
    if given is not None:
        best_a = resample_seq(given, n)
    else:
        best_a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    best_score = internal_score(best_a)
    print("initial best score = {:.12f}, length = {}".format(best_score, n), flush=True)

    # Quick deterministic/random initializations.
    shape_best_a = best_a.copy()
    shape_best_score = best_score
    shapes = make_initial_fast(n, int(seed) + 17, max_count=9)
    for a0 in shapes:
        sc = internal_score(a0)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < shape_best_score:
            shape_best_score = sc
            shape_best_a = a0.copy()

    if shape_best_score < best_score:
        best_score = shape_best_score
        best_a = shape_best_a.copy()
        print("shape best score = {:.12f}".format(best_score), flush=True)

    # Build pair-variable starts.
    starts = []

    def add_safe(v):
        v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if v.size != n - 1:
            return
        if np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
            starts.append(v)

    add_safe(pair_project(best_a, n))
    if given is not None:
        add_safe(pair_project(resample_seq(given, n), n))
    add_safe(pair_project(shape_best_a, n))

    for a0 in shapes[:4]:
        add_safe(pair_project(a0, n))

    M = n // 2
    rngp = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) + 101)

    # Noisy projection of best sequence.
    vbase = pair_project(best_a, n)
    vnoise = vbase.copy()
    zpart = vnoise[n - 1:n - 1 + M]
    zpart = np.clip(zpart + rngp.normal(0.0, 1.8, size=M), -Z_BOUND, Z_BOUND)
    vnoise[n - 1:n - 1 + M] = zpart
    add_safe(vnoise)

    # Random anti-orientation starts.
    for k in range(2):
        v = np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        signs = np.sign(rngp.random(M) - 0.5)
        signs[signs == 0.0] = 1.0
        z = 5.0 * signs
        v[n - 1:n - 1 + M] = z
        add_safe(v)

    # Smooth orientation patterns.
    t = np.arange(M, dtype=np.float64)
    for f, phase in ((1, 0.0), (2, 0.8), (3, 1.6)):
        v = np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        z = 5.0 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * t / float(M) + phase)
        v[n - 1:n - 1 + M] = np.clip(z, -Z_BOUND, Z_BOUND)
        add_safe(v)

    # Limit starts to preserve time.
    if len(starts) > 10:
        starts = starts[:10]

    # Pair L-BFGS main search.
    reserve_for_tail = 430.0
    remaining0 = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    per_start = max(20.0, min(210.0, (remaining0 - reserve_for_tail) / max(1, len(starts))))

    for i, v0 in enumerate(starts):
        now = time.monotonic()
        allow = min(per_start, deadline - now - reserve_for_tail)
        if allow < 15.0:
            break
        a1, s1 = optimize_pair_start(
            n,
            v0,
            now + allow,
            label="s{}".format(i),
        )
        if a1 is not None and np.isfinite(s1) and s1 < best_score:
            best_score = s1
            best_a = a1.copy()
            print("main pair selected score={:.12f}".format(best_score), flush=True)

    # Optional larger sequence search if time remains.
    now = time.monotonic()
    remaining = max(0.0, deadline - now)
    if remaining > 430.0:
        n_alt = 4096
        alt_budget = min(210.0, remaining - 250.0)
        if alt_budget > 60.0:
            a_alt0 = resample_seq(best_a, n_alt)
            v_alt = pair_project(a_alt0, n_alt)
            a1, s1 = optimize_pair_start(
                n_alt,
                v_alt,
                now + alt_budget,
                label="alt4096",
            )
            if a1 is not None and np.isfinite(s1) and s1 < best_score:
                best_score = s1
                best_a = a1.copy()
                print("larger n selected score={:.12f}, length={}".format(best_score, len(best_a)), flush=True)

    # Mirror descent polish.
    now = time.monotonic()
    if now < deadline - 90.0:
        allow = min(120.0, deadline - now - 40.0)
        if allow > 30.0:
            a1, s1 = optimize_mirror(best_a, now + allow, verbose=False)
            if a1 is not None and np.isfinite(s1) and s1 < best_score:
                best_score = s1
                best_a = a1.copy()
                print("mirror polish score={:.12f}".format(best_score), flush=True)

    # Local block polish.
    now = time.monotonic()
    if now < deadline - 25.0:
        allow = min(55.0, deadline - now - 10.0)
        if allow > 15.0:
            a1, s1 = local_polish(best_a, now + allow, seed=int(seed) + 31337)
            if a1 is not None and np.isfinite(s1) and s1 < best_score:
                best_score = s1
                best_a = a1.copy()
                print("local polish score={:.12f}".format(best_score), flush=True)

    best_a = normalize_positive(best_a, floor=0.0)
    out_list = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in best_a]

    try:
        final_eval = float(evaluate_sequence(out_list))
    except Exception:
        final_eval = float("inf")

    if not np.isfinite(final_eval):
        best_a = np.ones(2048, dtype=np.float64) / 2048.0
        out_list = [float(x) for x in best_a]
        final_eval = 2.0

    print("final internal score = {:.12f}".format(best_score), flush=True)
    print("final evaluated score = {:.12f}, length = {}".format(final_eval, len(out_list)), flush=True)
    return out_list
```
